Theorem 214.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the monte_carlo object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 419. The moduli space has dimension at most 319 with 4 connected components.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 419 is immediate from the convergence analysis property. For the inductive step, we decompose the monte_carlo structure using the convergence analysis theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 319. Each subproblem has a solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 4. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
